a) compute B matrix
b) compute bound
c) clear assignments
d) disallow assignment
f) fix variable 
g) go run bnb on this node
h) heuristic
l) change depth of this node
m) create branching training data
o) output current subproblem to QAPLIB file
p) change parameters
r) branching decision
s) evaluate a permutation
t) try bnb on all possible branching decisions
u) show U matrix 
v) unit tests 
/) change debug mode 
q) quit
